<input type='checkbox' name='article_531' value='https://thehackernews.com/2026/04/google-attributes-axios-npm-supply.html' /> 531 - <a href='https://www.google.com/search?q=thehackernews.com+Google+Attributes+Axios+npm+Supply+Chain+Attack+to+North+Korean+Group+UNC1069%EE%A0%82Apr+01%2C+2026Threat+Intelligence+%2F+Software+SecurityGoogle+has+formally+attributed++the+supply+chain+compromise++of+the+popular+Axios+npm+package+to+a+financially+motivated+North+Korean+threat+activity+cluster+tracked+as+UNC1069+.++%22We+have+attributed+the+attack+to+a+suspected+North+Korean+threat+actor+we+track+as+UNC1069%2C%22+John+Hultquist%2C+chief+analyst+at+Google+Threat+Intelligence+Group+%28GTIG%29%2C+told+The+Hacker+News+in+a+statement.++%22North+Korean+hackers+have+deep+experience+with+supply+chain+attacks%2C+which+they%27ve+historically+used+to+steal+cryptocurrency.+The+full+breadth+of+this+incident+is+still+unclear%2C+but+given+the+popularity+of+the+compromised+package%2C+we+expect+it+will+have+far+reaching+impacts.%22++The+development+comes+after+threat+actors+seized+control++of+the+package+maintainer%27s+npm+account+to+push+two+trojanized+versions+1.14.1+and+0.30.4+that+introduced+a+malicious+dependency+named+%22plain-crypto-js%22+that%27s+used+to+deliver+a+cross-platform+backdoor+capable+of+infecting+Windows%2C+ma...' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Search - </a> <a href='https://12ft.io/https://thehackernews.com/2026/04/google-attributes-axios-npm-supply.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google Attributes Axios npm Supply Chain Attack to North Korean Group UNC1069Apr 01, 2026Threat Intelligence / Software SecurityGoogle has formally attributed  the supply chain compromise  of the popular Axios npm package to a financially motivated North Korean threat activity cluster tracked as UNC1069 .
